## Normal srapp Candidate Console entrypoint accepted without tenant writes (2026-11-05)

**Status:** `NORMAL_SRAPP_CONSOLE_CACHE_ACCEPTED`, `CANDIDATE_CONSOLE_NORMAL_ENTRYPOINT_ACCEPTED`, `P0_SLICE_1_LIVE_FUNCTIONALLY_ACCEPTED`. This closes the normal-entrypoint gate in the preceding RecordCall/workflow acceptance checkpoint below; it does not reopen Candidate Core or establish clean-BSO or Production acceptance. `CLEAN_BSO_ACCEPTANCE_DEFERRED`, `P0_SOURCE_AHEAD_OF_FROZEN_RELEASE_ARTIFACT`, `BUSINESS_MVP_READY_FOR_PRODUCTION_FALSE`, `REAL_DATA_STILL_PROHIBITED`.

**Read-only diagnosis and classification:** `SRAPP_STALE_RESOURCE_NOT_REPRODUCED_NORMAL_ENTRYPOINT_CURRENT`. Fresh PAC identity and browser global context agreed on owner-approved VN LMV IT and exact `srapp`. Normal Candidate Management navigation already served the accepted resources on the first observation. The earlier stale response was not reproduced, and its historical root cause remains unproven; no browser-only, stale-publication, or platform-cache defect is asserted.

The published and current/unpublished Site Map both reference `subarea_bso_candidate_management` with `Url="$webresource:os_/bso/candidate-console.html"` and no authored version/query token. AppModule `appgraph`, `appmodulexmlmanaged`, and `configxml` were empty on the published read; navigation resolves through its associated `srapp` Site Map. The app's own `webResourceId` resolves to the platform default app icon, not the Candidate Console.

The browser wrapper used `pagetype=webresource&webresourceName=os_%2Fbso%2Fcandidate-console.html`. The platform's `getWebResourceUrl('os_/bso/candidate-console.html')` returned the exact iframe path:

```text
/%7b639294395670000177%7d/webresources/os_/bso/candidate-console.html
```

Its script request was:

```text
/%7b639294395670000177%7d/webresources/os_/bso/candidate-console.js?v=cb26a8d5a859
```

The HTML contains the relative `candidate-console.js?v=cb26a8d5a859` reference; the browser resolves it under the platform-generated versioned directory. The directory token decodes as .NET UTC ticks `2026-11-05T01:39:27.0000177Z`, consistent with the resource records' `ModifiedOn=2026-11-05T01:39:27Z`. This establishes the observed URL construction, not the cause of the earlier stale response.

**Exact SHA-256 reconciliation:**

| Layer | HTML | JavaScript |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Authored source / accepted source | `b922522c7c818ae28b30814bd6adafadb260759f437b5f3a149e117d92d95fba` | `cb26a8d5a859e9a72b27e25ee8220a4ad5704d4473dd37af0ee7b69ec0d55e53` |
| Current (`RetrieveUnpublishedMultiple`) | Same accepted HTML hash | Same accepted JS hash |
| Published definition | Same accepted HTML hash | Same accepted JS hash |
| Normal-wrapper effective response body | Same accepted HTML hash | Same accepted JS hash |
| Direct unversioned effective response body | Same accepted HTML hash | Same accepted JS hash |

Both definition paths returned `componentstate=0`. Browser response-body lengths were 16,483 / 119,245 bytes, HTTP 200. Normal-wrapper responses reported `fromServiceWorker=true`; read-only inspection of the platform `WebResources` CacheStorage found these exact versioned HTML/JS URLs with the same accepted hashes. Response headers included `Cache-Control: public`, with expiry in November 2027. The presence of caching is not evidence that these bytes are stale. Direct unversioned comparison used GET fetches only; neither acceptance iframe was manually redirected.

**No-write resolution and fresh-path acceptance:** Normal app reopen/navigation and full reload sufficed. A genuinely new browser tab was opened using only `main.aspx?appid=<exact srapp app id>`; the owner completed its sign-in manually. Candidate Management loaded through normal app navigation/default navigation. A subsequent normal-wrapper reload captured and hashed the actual HTML and JS responses above. A navigation-away/back observation reused an iframe and did not yield a complete response pair within the observation timeout; acceptance uses the successful reload response capture, not a substituted fetch. No cache disabling, cache clearing, service-worker unregister, token replacement, publication, or tenant correction was needed.

Candidate Management rendered correctly. Existing synthetic `CAND-00001066` was searched and opened using **Open**, without editing or invoking a business action. Its details showed **Round 1 Scheduled**. Scheduling showed one Round 1 **ACTIVE / SCHEDULED** row, `[DEV]` interviewer/location, Pending result, and **6 November 2026 10:00-11:00** in the browser's displayed local time. No Candidate, TA, or Location mutation occurred.

The complete 51-member Solution identity/behavior set matched current authored roots plus the package-backed generated assembly, API/input/output/package memberships, and existing app child. App role associations matched after resolving exported built-in role-template IDs to live role instances; no app/Solution membership drift was found. BSO's 60 privilege/depth entries and Reference Admin's 20 matched canonical role XML exactly. Reference Admin remained unassigned to users and teams. No role/security change occurred.

The sanitized trace contains no `CC_STAGE`/`CC_SERVICE_FAULT` marker, meaning the failure occurred before the first Dataverse service stage. It contains exactly one allowlisted Candidate Core rejection: `os_sr_cact rejected code=INVALID_CONTACT_TIME`. The only source of this code is the RecordCall input check in [Operation.cs](../solutions/CandidateCore/Operation.cs): `time.Kind != Utc || time > utcNow`, where `utcNow` comes from `DateTime.UtcNow` in [DataversePlugin.cs](../solutions/CandidateCore/DataversePlugin.cs). Trace timestamps are consistent with the future-time branch, but the `Kind` branch is not independently excluded. Minimum offline inspection: `ContactedAt` conversion in the input adapter, then `Operation.cs`. No source change, retry, trace-setting write, package/UI/publication/security write, or business dispatch occurred in this continuation. ## Masked lookup Guid.Empty clear correction validated offline (2026-11-05)

**Status:** `MASKED_OMISSION_GUID_CLEAR_RUNTIME_FAILED` (live failure retained), `GUID_TRANSPORT_DEFAULT_CLEAR_ROOT_CAUSE_REPRODUCED_OFFLINE`, `MASKED_GUID_EMPTY_CLEAR_SEMANTICS_IMPLEMENTED`, `PROFILE_LOOKUP_CLEAR_TRANSPORT_NORMALIZATION_VALIDATED`, `P0_PROFILE_CLEAR_FINAL_PACKAGE_CORRECTION_READY`. This section is offline source and local-artifact evidence only; deployment and runtime acceptance are not claimed here.

**Root cause proven offline:** A new SDK regression submits the exact 21-input Custom API shape with `ActionType=UpdateCandidate`, `ProfileFieldMask=AssignedTaId`, `AssignedTaId=Guid.Empty`, and platform defaults for every other optional input. Against the unmodified source it threw `INVALID_GUID_PARAMETER:AssignedTaId` from the shared request parser, the rule that rejected masked `Guid.Empty`. This is consistent with Dataverse materializing an omitted optional Guid input as `Guid.Empty` and with the live HTTP 400 / `0x80040265`; the live internal error category itself is not proven.

**Correction:** For UpdateCandidate's Position, Candidate Source, and Assigned TA lookups, `ProfileFieldMask` is the authoritative mutation intent. Unmasked absent/null/`Guid.Empty` values are preserved without validation or mutation. Masked absent, null, or `Guid.Empty` clears the lookup; a masked non-empty Guid is set only after the existing caller-context active-reference validation; malformed non-Guid values still reject with `INVALID_GUID_PARAMETER:<parameter>`. Required Name/Email, optional Phone/CV text, CreateCandidate, required-GUID, fingerprint version `1`, receipt, Console, and 21-input/five-output API metadata behavior is unchanged. Console writes for this correction: zero.

**Offline evidence:** The live-shaped regression now produces one Candidate update containing only `os_sr_cand_ta = null`, one UpdateCandidate Receipt, unchanged status/owner, and no Call Log, Status History, or Interview Schedule. Each lookup's masked omitted/null/`Guid.Empty` clears produce one shared fingerprint; unmasked `Guid.Empty` preserves and fingerprints differently; masked valid references are validated and set; inactive references and malformed values remain rejected. Historical Phone/CV and maskless receipt replays remain `DuplicateReplay` without a second update. The domain harness passes 236 assertions and the SDK harness 468.

**New local package (not deployed by this record):** Built from scratch outside the repository from HEAD `ecb8ea8` plus the uncommitted Candidate Core source correction; package `os_sr_cact_pkg` `1.0.0` SHA-256 `650c7d0f0c6c650b5c952e9bb9b96a499ec3c3e106a14a4d6eef158cc885dbe9`, embedded unsigned `lib/net462/os_sr_cact.dll` (`os_sr_cact` `1.0.0.0`) SHA-256 `cfb4aee88e5091f8f3fac78e89e2b4ca3577b93faf84b66b2cb5e57298ffbe50`. A reflection probe of that packaged DLL confirmed masked `Guid.Empty`/null/omitted Assigned TA clear equivalence and malformed-value rejection. The superseded artifact `219b90c2…` is not reused. Frozen managed `cf701740…`, frozen repository package `dbf60872…`, release manifests, ProductionReadiness pins and Solution version `1.0.0.1` are unchanged.

The offline `BsoRsProductionReadiness.Tests.ps1` currently fails its frozen canonical-source/root-component fingerprint assertion because this work intentionally changes tracked solution source without rebuilding the release candidate. Do not update the frozen release pins or claim readiness for these changes; a new release qualification requires its own authorization after live acceptance.

**Target and identity gates:**

- Read-only checks confirmed the exact VN LMV IT organization, environment, and URL; `srapp`; `os_sr_bso_rs_support`; active `os_sr_cact`; and `os_sr_cact_pkg`.
- The BSO identity previously accepted in Phase 5B was used:
  - enabled;
  - only direct role `BSO-RS Support - BSO` (60 privileges, with no Create/Write/Delete on any `os_sr_*` table);
  - only the default business-unit team, with no team roles;
  - no System Administrator or System Customizer;
  - Candidate Core execution and reference-table reads present.

  Result: `FINAL_SMOKE_BSO_IDENTITY_VERIFIED`.
- Browser `WhoAmI` matched that identity on the exact `srapp`. The Console loaded the Candidate Core path in DEV synthetic-only mode, and the fallback was unavailable. Result: `FINAL_SMOKE_BROWSER_IDENTITY_VERIFIED`.
- The deployment identity was not used for any Candidate operation.

**Lifecycle smoke:** Under the same BSO browser identity, through the Console UI, these four operations each returned `Applied`:

1. CreateCandidate;
2. RecordCall = Answered;
3. ProceedToScheduling;
4. MarkRound1Scheduled.

The data used existing `[DEV]` synthetic reference records and one `[SYNTHETIC-E2E]` Candidate with a `.invalid` email address. Server readback confirmed:

- a server-generated Candidate Number;
- owner = the initiating BSO;
- final status Round 1 Scheduled;
- exactly 1 Call Log, 3 Status History rows, 1 active Interview Schedule with the expected TA and Location, and 4 Operation Receipts matching the 4 CorrelationIds and transitions.

After a full browser reload, the same state, history, schedule, and Candidate Number were shown. Result: `FINAL_SMOKE_LIFECYCLE_PERSISTENCE_ACCEPTED`.

**Idempotency and concurrency:**

- Replaying RecordCall with the same CorrelationId and payload returned `DuplicateReplay`, with the original Call Log and history IDs.
- The same CorrelationId with a conflicting payload was rejected with `CORRELATION_CONFLICT`.
- A stale `ExpectedStatus` request was rejected with `STALE_EXPECTED_STATUS`.
- Readback afterwards: counts were still 1/3/1, Receipts still 4, no Receipt for the stale request, Candidate unchanged.

Results: `FINAL_SMOKE_IDEMPOTENCY_ACCEPTED` and `FINAL_SMOKE_CONCURRENCY_ACCEPTED`.

**Direct-write negatives:** Each was attempted once under the same identity, and each was denied with HTTP 403 by Dataverse privilege enforcement:

- Candidate Create (`prvCreateos_sr_cand`);
- Candidate Update (`prvWriteos_sr_cand`);
- Call Log Create (`prvCreateos_sr_call`);
- Status History Create (`prvCreateos_sr_csh`);
- Interview Schedule Create (`prvCreateos_sr_intv`).

No rows were created. A final reconciliation found the role, team, and privilege state unchanged. Result: `FINAL_SMOKE_BSO_SECURITY_ACCEPTED`.

**Retention:** No rollback was needed or performed. The synthetic Candidate and its records are kept as Test evidence. Identifiers are held in session evidence and omitted here.

**Remaining gates (external/business/Production only):**

- explicit Production target approval;
- real-data authorization;
- privacy, audit, and DLP approval where required;
- Production BSO user and access confirmation;
- backup and recovery ownership;
- support and escalation ownership;
- the ATSugg data-migration decision, if migration is required.

No further technical rehearsal phase is required by this decision.

## VN LMV IT publication-state ambiguity (2026-11-03)

During a PAC help check, `pac solution publish` was accidentally invoked against VN LMV IT and displayed `Publishing All Customizations...`. The client was terminated after about 30 seconds, and server-side completion is unknown: `VN_LMV_IT_PUBLISHALL_COMPLETION_UNKNOWN`.

Read-only reconciliation found:

- all five Candidate Console resources still satisfy published == current == effective == captured DEV baseline, with HTTP 200;
- `srapp` and its sitemap remain Published;
- the sitemap modification time matches the prior snapshot.

Together these give `VN_LMV_IT_KNOWN_CANDIDATE_CONSOLE_BASELINE_INTACT`. There is no proof either way about unrelated unpublished customizations. No corrective, compensating, or undo write was attempted. The frozen artifact predates the incident and remains accepted.

Classification: `VN_LMV_IT_PUBLICATION_STATE_AMBIGUITY_RECORDED`. VN LMV IT is quarantined from writes for this Test continuation. The owner should review this before any future VN LMV IT source-authoring operation. It does not block the LMV BI DEV rehearsal.

The existing `os_sr_intv` table was extended in VN LMV IT; no second scheduling table was created. Its optional schedule lifecycle Choice supports Proposed, Scheduled, Cancelled, and Superseded. Existing rows with no lifecycle value are displayed and treated as Scheduled for backward compatibility. A reschedule preserves the old row as Superseded, stores the required reason and changed timestamp, creates a Proposed replacement, then returns the Candidate to `Need Call Candidate`. The Proposed row is not current/active while the Candidate is being re-contacted. On a later successful Schedule Round 1 action, the BSO explicitly reselects the TA and Location and re-enters the date/time; the Proposed row is read back and promoted to Scheduled rather than duplicated. Cancellation preserves the row as Cancelled, stores its reason/time, and returns the Candidate to `Need Call Candidate`. Resulted Pass/Fail schedules, terminal Candidates, ambiguous schedule states, and multiple active/proposed rows fail closed. All Round 1 rows remain visible newest first.

The current Solution and Publisher match the approved unmanaged `os_sr_bso_rs_support` / `os_bso_rs_support` / `os` target. The adopted `srapp` remains Active/Unpublished, the existing Site Map remains directly associated and Published, its ETag is available, and the expected System Administrator and System Customizer roles remain associated.

- The live direct inventory has 13 records with nominal counts of four type-1 entities, four type-60 forms, four type-26 views, and one type-62 Site Map. Exact object identity reconciliation fails: all eight selected Information forms and public active views match their approved component IDs, but none of the four type-1 records matches an expected reference-table metadata ID. The four entity component IDs therefore remain unresolved; do not treat the count/type inventory as the exact approved 12-component set.
- The app-component records return an empty `rootappmodulecomponentid` and null `rootcomponentbehavior` for the forms and views (and no populated root ID for the other direct records). The forms/views themselves resolve to their intended parent tables by their form/view metadata. This establishes that parent metadata exists, but not that the app-component hierarchy links those assets beneath the intended entity components.
- `ValidateApp` succeeds, with zero blocking errors and nine warnings. Eight asset warnings have null messages and CRM code `0`; each has one required type-1 entity component with the expected schema name (`os_sr_ta`, `os_sr_pos`, `os_sr_csrc`, or `os_sr_loc`) for its form or view. The ninth warning has CRM code `-2147155691` and says “Entity doesn’t reference a form or view. App users will see all forms and views.” There is no Site Map blocker.
- Reproduction of the local exact-composition check fails at `Assert-ReferenceUiComponentState -RequireComposed` with `Unexpected direct AppModule component exists.` This is the first failing predicate in the current reconciliation order. The Phase 2D.3B wrapper did not retain its inner exception, so the reproduced failure is the strongest available explanation, not proof of the exact historical exception. The current `Get-ReferenceUiValidationState` warning predicate only counts the exact text `App does not reference at least one entity`; its count is zero here and it does not recognize the actual entity/form/view warning. Do not relax the exact component gate or use that warning count as evidence of successful linkage.
- The Site Map transformer produces the approved Settings > Administration navigation for TA Directory, Positions, Candidate Sources, and Locations. Semantic-preservation and duplicate-navigation-ID checks pass; the current published XML still contains only the placeholder node, so a change is required. The fresh ETag is available, but the Site Map remains unpatched because exact app composition failed.
- The most recent successful guarded diagnostic reported **30 GET**, **0 POST**, **0 PATCH**, and **0 writes**. Other diagnostic attempts also used only GET; their failed local report formatting did not retain counters, so a cumulative Phase 2D.3C GET total is **UNKNOWN**. AddAppComponents, Site Map PATCH, other POST, PublishXml, app publish, role changes, and retries all remain **0** for this diagnostic.
- **Outcome: LIVE_STATE_INCONSISTENCY.** Do not repeat AddAppComponents or patch/publish `srapp`. Resolve the four unexpected type-1 component identities and the form/view root linkage using a separately authorized, freshly guarded plan before another composition attempt.

## Phase 2D.3F Update — GET-Only Entity Identity Diagnostic

After fixing the local diagnostic-mode process-pin allow-list, one fresh guarded diagnostic completed. It restored target guards from the authoritative runbook in the short-lived process, verified PAC/Az/Dataverse WhoAmI agreement, and set the expected-user pin only after identity agreement. The exact VN LMV IT target, unmanaged Solution `os_sr_bso_rs_support`, Publisher `os_bso_rs_support` / prefix `os`, and existing `srapp` identity/state were confirmed. No interactive login or environment switch occurred.

- The direct app inventory reported 13 components: one Site Map, four type-1 entities, four forms, four views, and no other component types. `srapp` remains Active / Unpublished. The existing app-aware Site Map was resolved as `srapp`; an ETag was available. The observed role associations remain System Administrator and System Customizer.
- Each type-1 component had a present, syntactically valid object ID, matched the queried `entities.entityid`, returned HTTP 200 with exactly one row, and matched its component-to-app association. However, all four rows returned the logical name `entity`, not the expected `os_sr_ta`, `os_sr_pos`, `os_sr_csrc`, and `os_sr_loc`. The approved-payload entity IDs were absent from the diagnostic projection, so payload-ID equality comparisons were unavailable. The structural identity-contract check passed, but the approved-name check failed with `ENTITY_LOGICALNAME_UNEXPECTED`; aggregate result: `ENTITY_MAPPING_WRONG_LOGICALNAME`. Exact intended table identity remains unverified and the composition gate fails.
- The four `Information` Main forms and four expected public active views were independently found by name and were active. This does not repair or prove the entity mapping. A GET-only `RetrieveAppComponents` attempt returned HTTP 404; this does not establish that the app has no components and was not used to override the direct-inventory evidence.
- `ValidateApp` returned success with zero blocking errors and nine warnings: eight `UNRESOLVED_REQUIRED_COMPONENT` warnings for the forms and views, plus a non-blocking functional warning with code `-2147155691`. A successful validator result does not satisfy the failed logical-name identity gate.
- The deterministic local Site Map semantic projection used the freshly retrieved XML: the existing placeholder Area/Group/SubArea would become Settings > Administration with the four approved reference-table entries. The projection was not patched. Since the exact entity mapping gate failed, the result is not readiness to update navigation; no Site Map XML, ETag, or roles were changed.
- **Outcome: FAILED_VALIDATION / NOT READY FOR SITE MAP PATCH.** Counts: **32 GET** (including the 404 `RetrieveAppComponents` request), **0 AddAppComponents POST**, **0 RemoveAppComponents**, **0 Site Map PATCH**, **0 other POST**, **0 PublishXml**, **0 app publish**, **0 role changes**, **0 retries**. `WritesMayHaveOccurred=false`.
- Focused offline validation passed **118** app-composition assertions, **550** provisioning/recovery assertions, **11** readiness assertions, and `git diff --check`. The diagnostic-mode process pin is limited to the guarded read-only diagnostic path. No commit or push occurred.

## Facts

- Sprint 02B.1 added a Position-only `-CreateOnlyRecovery` path with a fixed SHA-256 payload guard, explicit expected-user and authorization-reference gates, one-POST/no-retry controls, and GET-only reconciliation. Its initial synthetic suites passed 446 provisioning/recovery, 32 app-composition, and 11 readiness assertions. The mode was subsequently used once under separate owner authorization in Sprint 02C; the response status was not retained, and its immediate reconciliation reported Position absent. At that time no further Position create/recovery POST was authorized; Sprint 02D later separately authorized a conditional publication, which was not executed because publication state remained UNKNOWN.
- The repository initially contained only `BSO-RS-Support-Phase0-Blueprint.md` plus Git metadata. The blueprint is a draft for design review, not a record of deployed resources.
- Phase 0 created the documentation and governance foundation. Phase 1B created only the project Publisher and an empty unmanaged Solution in the explicitly selected VN LMV IT Production environment for internal IT development.
- Verified Publisher: **BSO-RS-Support Publisher**, unique name `os_bso_rs_support`, customization prefix `os`, option value prefix `10001`, ID `ee73e9f3-87cb-f111-a05a-000d3a64b349`.
- Verified Solution: **BSO-RS-Support**, unique name `os_sr_bso_rs_support`, version `1.0.0.0`, unmanaged, ID `63f48907-88cb-f111-a05a-000d3a64b349`, linked to the project Publisher.
- VN LMV IT is a Production environment explicitly selected for this internal IT development scope. Its URL and environment/organization IDs were verified during execution but are not recorded in this repository.
- Phase 1D-C owner approval authorized exactly `os_sr_ta`, including plural display name **TA Directories**. Dataverse Web API created the table and its two approved name/email columns using `MSCRM.SolutionUniqueName: os_sr_bso_rs_support`, then published only that table. No additional project tables, business records, custom relationships, alternate keys, roles, apps, flows, or Azure resources were created by this deployment.
- Independent metadata GETs verified **TA Directory / TA Directories**, `OrganizationOwned`, primary GUID `os_sr_taid` (`Uniqueidentifier`, primary ID), primary name `os_sr_ta_nm` (**TA Name**, String/Text, 200, `ApplicationRequired`, `IsPrimaryName=true`), and `os_sr_ta_eml` (**TA Email**, String/Email, 254, `ApplicationRequired`). Standard `statecode/statuscode` exist; no custom `is_active` or unintended custom columns/alternate keys exist. See [Data Model](DATA_MODEL.md).
- Solution membership is verified through the table root component with `rootcomponentbehavior=0` (**Include Subcomponents**). Separate attribute-component rows are not present or required for this whole-table inclusion. The initial single Apply invocation exited with a false-negative membership-verifier error after both creates and table-scoped publication; only read-only reconciliation followed. The local verifier was corrected and independent read-back passed. No creation retry, deletion, recreation, or association repair was performed.
- Phase 1C covers the wider 15-table design. The approved TA, Position, Candidate Source, and Location reference-table slices are verified as implemented; remaining schema choices and components stay proposed. Phase 1D-B's earlier absence/empty-Solution checks are historical pre-creation evidence. At the earlier Phase 1D-D check, inventory contained only `os_sr_ta`; that is no longer the current inventory.
- Phase 1D-D Apply and all three plural labels were explicitly approved by the project owner on 20 October 2026. The single `os_sr_pos` Apply invocation reached the first `POST EntityDefinitions` attempt and received HTTP `401 Unauthorized`; the script stopped with exit code 1 and reported `WritesMayHaveOccurred=true`. No Apply was attempted for `os_sr_csrc` or `os_sr_loc`. The subsequent read-only reconciliation at that time found no `os_sr_pos` metadata and confirmed the project inventory was exactly `os_sr_ta`; that historical absence was later superseded by the Sprint 02C.1 verified Position state above. **Root cause is UNKNOWN.** The failed request's body/code, WWW-Authenticate challenge, request/correlation IDs, timestamp, and token expiry were not captured. Transcript ordering shows the typed confirmation and failure occurred within seconds, with successful GETs before the POST. Do not retry or recreate Position.
- Read-only effective privilege calls for the authenticated user returned Global depth for `prvCreateEntity`, `prvCreateAttribute`, `prvCreateCustomization`, and `prvPublishCustomization`; a missing named metadata-create privilege is not supported by evidence. A later in-memory token inspection confirmed correct tenant, Dataverse audience, delegated scope, and validity at that later time, but it is not evidence about the failed token. Environment auditing remains disabled, retention remains 30 days, user-access auditing remains disabled, and the security-role snapshot hash matches pre-Apply. No business records or security-role changes were made. Location's requiredness/name source remains [Room/Resource Email](DATAVERSE_SCHEMA_SPECIFICATION.md): metadata `None`, with conditional booking validation reserved for the future operation.
- The provisioner now validates token tenant/audience/scope/expiry, refreshes with supported Az.Accounts when less than five minutes remain before any request (including after confirmation), and captures sanitized HTTP status/error/challenge/request/correlation/path/timing diagnostics without tokens/full headers. This is preventive hardening; it does not establish the historical 401 cause.
- Sprint 02C.1 fresh live reconciliation made 33 GET requests and zero writes. It verified project tables `os_sr_ta` and `os_sr_pos`; Position matches the approved display labels, organization ownership, primary GUID/name, String/Text primary-name column, length 200, and ApplicationRequired level. No unexpected custom Position attributes, alternate keys, custom relationships, or unmapped Entity/Attribute Solution components were found; existing table metadata remained unchanged. Solution `os_sr_bso_rs_support` and Publisher `os_bso_rs_support` (`os`) remain verified. This establishes the current state, not which historical request created it. A separate GET-only app dry run made 12 GETs and observed `srapp` Active/Unpublished (`componentstate=1`) and the Site Map with no unpublished layer reported. No application write occurred in this sprint.
- Sprint 02C.1 locally hardened exception-path diagnostics in the provisioner. Future failures retain only validated HTTP status, allowlisted error code, fixed safe summary, validated request/correlation GUIDs, allowlisted authentication-challenge properties, exception/transport category, relative API path, dispatch/write-may-have-occurred Booleans, and reconciliation outcome. Missing status is serialized as `UNKNOWN`; terminal classification distinguishes 401, 403, validation 4xx, 429, 5xx, timeout, transport failure, and unknown HTTP failure. Raw bodies, full headers, tokens, identities, and full URLs are not serialized. The synthetic provisioning/recovery suite passes 518 assertions; the separate app-composition and readiness suites pass 32 and 11 assertions. These changes cannot recover diagnostics from the earlier operation.

## Blockers and decisions
- Phase 2C-R3 owner decision, 21 October 2026: officially adopt existing AppModule **BSO-RS-Support**, actual unique name `srapp`, ID `3709bb7c-28cd-f111-a05c-70a8a581cbfa`, in Solution `os_sr_bso_rs_support` in VN LMV IT. This is an app-only naming exception; `srapp` supersedes the prior `os_srapp` proposal. No additional AppModule, rename, or recreation is needed or authorized. Phase 2C is closed by adoption, not by a successful Create. Solution membership is verified by the type-80 root; the AppModule's own Publisher lookup remains **UNKNOWN** and is not inferred from the Solution Publisher. At that historical checkpoint, the Phase 2D plan was not authorized; later Phase 2D.3 authorization and the blocked preflight are recorded above. Phase 2C-R2 is the adoption-time composition baseline. The subsequent Phase 2D-P GET-only verification below reconfirmed the AppModule and Site Map states, empty direct component list, role associations, type 80/62 roots, and type 10091 identity UNKNOWN. Exact TA Main form and Active public view IDs are documented in the [App Manifest](MODEL_DRIVEN_APP_MANIFEST.md). No Phase 2D operation occurred at the time of that checkpoint.
- Phase 2D-P read-only verification, 21 October 2026: a target-identity-checked DryRun made 12 GETs and zero PATCH/POST requests. It reconfirmed AppModule `srapp` Active/Unpublished, Site Map `srapp` Published with placeholder XML and no separate unpublished layer returned, TA ObjectTypeCode `11005`, Main Information form `e076f089-e35b-46f3-b123-9cf0463759e5` (type 2, Active/Published), and public Active TA Directories view `2cb98672-8176-4337-af5e-d6aabcac8f2d` (query type 0, Active/Published). The live Solution export still has one `AppModuleSiteMap.SiteMapUniqueName=srapp` entry; direct `appmodulecomponents` remains empty. Live `$metadata` verified AddAppComponents parameters `AppId: Edm.Guid` and `Components: Collection(mscrm.crmbaseentity)` plus primary-key properties for Entity, Site Map, Form, and View. AppModule Publisher remains UNKNOWN; type 10091 exact identity remains UNKNOWN. The proposed Site Map XML preserves its existing three node IDs. No app composition, publication, role/audit change, business-row read, or Dataverse write occurred. Phase 2D readiness was BLOCKED pending separate authorization and runtime behavior checks at that time; the later Phase 2D.1 result is recorded above.

Schema owner approval and remaining field/choice decisions; dedicated Dev/Test availability and production-environment governance; BSO connector identity and mailbox authorization; TA and room calendar permissions; DLP; storage/retention and privacy; LEGO API contract; ATSugg schema/data quality; capacity and production operations. Details are in [Product Decisions](PRODUCT_DECISIONS.md) and [Dataverse Schema Specification](DATAVERSE_SCHEMA_SPECIFICATION.md).

## Current next authorized action

The Phase 2D.3C `LIVE_STATE_INCONSISTENCY` conclusion is withdrawn as unsupported: `appmodulecomponent.objectid` for a type-1 component must be resolved through `entities.entityid` to `logicalname`; it must not be compared with `EntityDefinitions.MetadataId` or `appmodulecomponentid`. The Phase 2D.3D HTTP 400 occurred because the resolver used unsupported `GET /entities(<guid>)`; Phase 2D.3E uses the `entities` collection. The latest Phase 2D.3F run passed the target and PAC/Az/WhoAmI guards, then failed the exact identity gate because every queried entity row returned logical name `entity`. It made 32 GETs and zero writes; `RetrieveAppComponents` returned HTTP 404. Although `ValidateApp` had zero blocking errors, the four type-1 components do not resolve to the approved tables. Do not repeat AddAppComponents, patch the Site Map, or publish `srapp`. First determine the supported entity identity/read contract from the observed collection response without weakening the fail-closed check; any further live diagnostic requires a fresh guarded scope.
